Third-party licenses of the packages compiled into the Comet build (guest/comet/comet-aarch64).
Comet itself: Apache-2.0 (LICENSE-comet). Its protocol definitions (proto/): CC0-1.0.
Collected from the package sources cargo downloaded for the build; the list with versions is in CRATES.tsv.
